  Gloss: This means approaching one's work with a lukewarm, unenthusiastic manner, doing what is asked without much eagerness or energy behind the effort.
- **verbal** (`verbal#1`, cut-off 3) by [eloquent](../../../traits/instructions/eloquent.json): Sonnet 2 ("Both involve fluent, well-chosen language, but eloquent adds grace and persuasiveness while verbal stresses precision and avoiding vague wording, so each has something the other lacks."), Opus 3 ("Both describe fluent, skillful command of language; eloquent carries verbal facility further, adding grace and persuasive power.")
  Gloss: This means choosing words with care, speaking fluently and precisely, and reaching for the apt phrase instead of settling for vague or clumsy language.
- **vivacious** (`vivacious#1`, cut-off 3) by [extraverted (Big Five)](../../../traits/instructions/extraverted_big_five.json): Sonnet 2 ("Both involve lively, energetic, enthusiastic engagement with others, but extraversion is broader (assertiveness, sociability, excitement-seeking, warmth) while vivacious centers on expressive animation, so each has features the other lacks."), Opus 3 ("Vivacious is the lively, cheerful, energetic side of extraversion; extraversion is the broader trait that also covers sociability, assertiveness and excitement-seeking.")
  Gloss: This means bringing lively energy and animation to every conversation and room, with a quick smile, expressive gestures, and enthusiasm that rarely dims for long.
- **well-bred** (`well_bred#1`, cut-off 3) by [polite](../../../traits/instructions/polite.json): Sonnet 2 ("Both concern courteous manners, but well-bred adds refinement and consistent propriety even when unobserved, while polite is about civil everyday behaviors; polite is the narrower, more surface-level concept."), Opus 3 ("Well-bred is polite courtesy carried further into refinement and propriety at all times, so it is the same concept at a greater degree and with a wider scope.")
  Gloss: This means keeping polite, refined manners in every exchange, measuring one's speech and conduct by what is considerate and proper even when no one is watching or expecting it.
- **withdrawing** (`withdrawing#1`, cut-off 3) by [reserved](../../../traits/instructions/reserved.json): Sonnet 2 ("Both involve keeping feelings private and keeping distance from others, but 'withdrawing' adds passivity in social initiative (avoiding small talk and new company) and 'reserved' adds politeness and emotional concealment, so each has something the other lacks."), Opus 3 ("Both describe keeping feelings to oneself and holding others at a distance. Withdrawing leans more toward social non-initiation, and reserved leans more toward concealing emotions, but these are differences of emphasis on one concept.")
  Gloss: This means keeping one's feelings and opinions to oneself, holding back from small talk and new company, and letting others come to one rather than reaching toward them.
- **zany** (`zany#1`, cut-off 3) by [playful](../../../traits/instructions/playful.json): Sonnet 2 ("Both involve humor and fun, but zany is wildly silly, eccentric, over-the-top behavior while playful is a broader, gentler lightheartedness and whimsy; each can occur without the other."), Opus 3 ("Both are about fun and humor, but zany is an extreme of wild, absurd silliness, while playful is a milder lightheartedness that need not be eccentric or chaotic.")
  Gloss: This means acting in wildly silly, eccentric ways, cracking jokes at odd moments, striking absurd poses, and treating every situation as a chance for comic mayhem.
- **zealless** (`zealless#1`, cut-off 3) by [burned-out](../../../traits/instructions/burned_out.json): Sonnet 2 ("Both involve going through the motions with little energy or care, but burned-out implies prior investment, exhaustion and cynicism, while zealless is a flat absence of interest without that history."), Opus 3 ("Both describe going through the motions without energy or care, but burned-out adds that the work once mattered and that the person is now exhausted and cynical, a narrower case of zealless.")
  Gloss: This means going through the motions on every task, bringing no energy or interest, doing what is asked at the minimum and feeling nothing about whether it matters or how it turns out.

## Both ends similar (orthogonal to the pair?) (5 rows)

Decision 13 (Roger's rule): the relation call marked both members of a recorded pair (every corner of a triangle or simplex) similar, so the candidate is probably orthogonal to the pair's axis: neither member was judged or may cover it.  Cosines to the candidate; overlap readings where any are on record.

- **bright** (`bright#1`, covered by [quick-witted](../../../traits/instructions/quick_witted.json), cut-off 3).  Gloss: This means grasping ideas quickly, reasoning sharply, and turning a clever remark or answer without effort, treating every problem as something to be understood on the spot.
  - pair: [analytical](../../../traits/instructions/analytical.json) (cosine 0.118); [intuitive](../../../traits/instructions/intuitive.json) (cosine 0.253)
- **prosperous** (`prosperous#1`, covered by [wealthy](../../../traits/instructions/wealthy.json), cut-off 3).  Gloss: This means having ample money and being financially well-off.
  - pair: [new money](../../../traits/instructions/new_money.json) (cosine 0.446); [old money](../../../traits/instructions/old_money.json) (cosine 0.394)
- **smart** (`smart#1`, covered by [quick-witted](../../../traits/instructions/quick_witted.json), cut-off 3).  Gloss: This means grasping ideas quickly, reasoning sharply through problems, and turning a clever remark on the spot when the moment calls for it.
  - pair: [analytical](../../../traits/instructions/analytical.json) (cosine 0.127); [intuitive](../../../traits/instructions/intuitive.json) (cosine 0.272)
- **employed** (`employed#1`, new, cut-off 3).  Gloss: This means having a paid job working for someone.
  - pair: [company-loyal](../../../traits/instructions/company_loyal.json) (cosine 0.257); [job-hopping](../../../traits/instructions/job_hopping.json) (cosine 0.177)
- **knowledgeable** (`knowledgeable#1`, new, cut-off 3).  Gloss: This means drawing on broad and deep knowledge to answer questions with confidence and authority, speaking as someone who has mastered the subject rather than guessing at it.
  - pair: [generalist](../../../traits/instructions/generalist.json) (cosine 0.345); [specialist](../../../traits/instructions/specialist.json) (cosine 0.321)
